} else if(temp < 38.5) { return "MILD FEVER"; } else if(temp < 39.5) { return "HIGH FEVER"; } else { return "CRITICAL"; } } // ===================================== // CSV Download // ===================================== void handleCSV() { server.sendHeader( "Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=medical_logs.csv" ); server.send( 200, "text/csv", csvData ); } the above reqired blocks in”. Finally, they asked: “(alternate/edited variant of an earlier question) [IMAGE ATTACHMENT file_id=file_000000007fc47207a0e56067530752a0, name=bd921a77-7e59-40a6-827d-98d72eb9463d.png, mime=image/png, 1916x964px, size=19912 bytes] — file referenced in export manifest (.dat); binary not included in provided export why this happeining and i need same animation as the above html file i submited and i need charching icon in patien watch name”.

### Suggestions & Next Steps

- Power the ESP32 from a clean 3.3 V rail: ≥100 µF bulk + 100 nF ceramic at the VDD pins, and a separate regulator for servos/motors — brownouts masquerade as random firmware bugs.
- Respect boot strapping pins (GPIO 0/2/12 on classic ESP32, GPIO 2/8/9 on C3): no external pulls that fight the straps, and never drive GPIO12 high at reset on 5 V-tolerant modules.
- Enable the hardware watchdog (esp_task_wdt) around your main loop and use deep-sleep with RTC retention for battery nodes.
- Derive A/B/C Steinhart–Hart coefficients from three known temperature points with a calibrated reference (ice point, room temp, ~50 °C oil bath) rather than trusting datasheet nominal constants.
- Keep excitation current low (<100 µA) and average ADC over ≥16 samples to keep self-heating error below 0.1 °C for 100k NTC @ 25 °C.
- Run `tsc --noEmit` in CI, pin exact dependency versions, and add a .env.example documenting required VITE_* variables; guard clipboard/localStorage calls behind HTTPS/feature checks.
- Regenerate this archive after every ChatGPT export (Settings → Data controls → Export) and diff against the previous INDEX.md to catch missing turns.
- Lock in the winning decision from this chat into your project docs/repo before the next ChatGPT session; context window loss is the main failure mode.

```cpp
    {

        json += "\"name\":\"" + patientNames[i] + "\",";
        json += "\"tempC\":" + String(patientTempC[i],1) + ",";
        json += "\"tempF\":" + String(patientTempF[i],1) + ",";
        json += "\"status\":\"" + patientStatus[i] + "\"";

        json += "}";

        if(i < TOTAL_PATIENTS - 1)
        {
            json += ",";
        }
    }

    json += "]";

    server.send(200, "application/json", json);
}
```

------------------------------------------------------------------------
That error means your `setup()` and `loop()` functions got deleted, broken, or trapped inside another function accidentally.

Arduino requires THESE TWO sacred rituals:

```cpp
void setup()
{
}

void loop()
{
}
```

Without them the compiler basically says:

> “Interesting. You gave me decorative HTML but no firmware brain.”

Very human situation honestly.

---

# WHY THIS HAPPENED

You probably:
- deleted part of the file accidentally
- pasted `handleRoot()` inside another function
- missed a `}` brace
- broke the structure before `setup()` or `loop()`

So linker cannot find them anymore.

---

# FIX IT FAST

# 1. FIND THIS

```cpp
void setup()
```

AND THIS

```cpp
void loop()
```

If they are missing:
PASTE THEM BACK.

---

# 2. USE THIS CLEAN FRAME

PASTE THIS ENTIRE BLOCK BELOW YOUR GLOBAL VARIABLES:

```cpp
void setup()
{
    Serial.begin(115200);

    pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

    WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

    while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
    {
        delay(500);
    }

    server.on("/", handleRoot);

    server.begin();
}

void loop()
{
    server.handleClient();

    static unsigned long previousRead = 0;

    if (millis() - previousRead >= 3000)
    {
        previousRead = millis();

        for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
        {
            float tempC = readTemperatureC(i);

            patientTempC[i] = tempC;

            patientTempF[i] =
            (tempC * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;

            patientStatus[i] =
            getHealthStatus(tempC);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 3. VERY IMPORTANT

AFTER `loop()` ENDS:

You MUST see this:

```cpp
}
```

ONLY ONE FINAL BRACE.

If braces mismatch:
- compiler chaos
- fake errors
- undefined reference
- functions disappear into dimensional void

---

# 4. FILE STRUCTURE SHOULD LOOK LIKE THIS

Correct structure:

```cpp
GLOBAL VARIABLES

FUNCTION PROTOTYPES

setup()

loop()

selectChannel()

readTemperatureC()

getHealthStatus()

handleRoot()

handleCSV()

handleEdit()
```

NOT:

```cpp
setup()
{
   handleRoot()
   {
```

That becomes cursed nesting.

- linker errors gone

- firmware builds again

Right now your project isn’t failing because of HTML anymore.

It’s failing because the ESP8266 literally cannot find reality’s entry points.

# 1. REQUIRED FUNCTION

```cpp id="xj5s1j"
void selectChannel(int channel)
{
    digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

    digitalWrite(S1,
                 (channel >> 1) & 1);

    digitalWrite(S2,
                 (channel >> 2) & 1);

    digitalWrite(S3,
                 (channel >> 3) & 1);
}
```

---

# 2. REQUIRED FUNCTION

Paste BELOW that:

```cpp id="d5e7qe"
float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
    selectChannel(patient);

    delay(5);

    float averageADC = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

        delay(5);
    }

    averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

    if (averageADC <= 1)
    {
        return -100;
    }

    float resistance =
        SERIES_RESISTOR /
        ((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

    float temperatureK =
        1.0 /
        (
            (1.0 /
            (NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15))
            +
            (
                log(
                    resistance /
                    NOMINAL_RESISTANCE
                )
                /
                BETA_COEFFICIENT
            )
        );

    float temperatureC =
        temperatureK - 273.15;

    temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];

    return temperatureC;
}
```

---

# 3. REQUIRED FUNCTION

Paste BELOW that:

```cpp id="h2yrbz"
String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
    if(temp < 35.0)
    {
        return "LOW";
    }
    else if(temp < 37.5)
    {
        return "NORMAL";
    }
    else if(temp < 38.5)
    {
        return "MILD FEVER";
    }
    else if(temp < 39.5)
    {
        return "HIGH FEVER";
    }
    else
    {
        return "CRITICAL";
    }
}
```

#define S1 D2

void handleData()
{
    String json = "[";

    for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
    {
        json += "{";

        json += "\"tempC\":" +
        String(patientTempC[i],1) + ",";

            json += ",";

// =====================================
// Edit Patient Data
// =====================================

void handleEdit()
{
    if (server.hasArg("id"))
    {
        int id =
            server.arg("id").toInt();

        if (id >= 0 &&
            id < TOTAL_PATIENTS)
        {
            // Change Name
            if (server.hasArg("name"))
            {
                String newName =
                    server.arg("name");

                if (newName.length() > 0)
                {
                    patientNames[id] =
                        newName;
                }
            }

            // Change Calibration
            if (server.hasArg("offset"))
            {
                String offsetValue =
                    server.arg("offset");

                if (offsetValue.length() > 0)
                {
                    calibrationOffset[id] =
                        offsetValue.toFloat();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    server.sendHeader("Location", "/");

    server.send(303);
}
void setup()
{
    Serial.begin(115200);

    pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

    WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

    while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
    {
        delay(500);
    }

    server.on("/", handleRoot);
    server.on("/data", handleData);

    server.begin();
}

void loop()
{
    server.handleClient();

    static unsigned long previousRead = 0;

    if (millis() - previousRead >= 3000)
    {
        previousRead = millis();

        for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
        {
            float tempC = readTemperatureC(i);

            patientTempC[i] = tempC;

            patientTempF[i] =
            (tempC * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;

            patientStatus[i] =
            getHealthStatus(tempC);
        }
    }
}
void selectChannel(int channel)
{
    digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

    digitalWrite(S1,
                 (channel >> 1) & 1);

    digitalWrite(S2,
                 (channel >> 2) & 1);

    digitalWrite(S3,
                 (channel >> 3) & 1);
}
float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
    selectChannel(patient);

    delay(5);

    float averageADC = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

        delay(5);
    }

    averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

    if (averageADC <= 1)
    {
        return -100;
    }

    float resistance =
        SERIES_RESISTOR /
        ((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

    float temperatureK =
        1.0 /
        (
            (1.0 /
            (NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15))
            +
            (
                log(
                    resistance /
                    NOMINAL_RESISTANCE
                )
                /
                BETA_COEFFICIENT
            )
        );

    float temperatureC =
        temperatureK - 273.15;

    temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];

    return temperatureC;
}
String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
    if(temp < 35.0)
    {
        return "LOW";
    }
    else if(temp < 37.5)
    {
        return "NORMAL";
    }
    else if(temp < 38.5)
    {
        return "MILD FEVER";
    }
    else if(temp < 39.5)
    {
        return "HIGH FEVER";
    }
    else
    {
        return "CRITICAL";
    }
}
// =====================================
// CSV Download
// =====================================

void handleCSV()
{
    server.sendHeader(
        "Content-Disposition",
        "attachment; filename=medical_logs.csv"
    );

    server.send(
        200,
        "text/csv",
        csvData
    );
}
 merge the above reqired blocks in this code

```cpp
const float BETA_COEFFICIENT = 3950.0;

String csvData =

pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
{
delay(500);
}

server.on("/", handleRoot);
server.on("/data", handleData);
server.on("/csv", handleCSV);

server.begin();
}

void loop()
{
server.handleClient();

static unsigned long previousRead = 0;

if (millis() - previousRead >= 2000)
{
previousRead = millis();

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
float tempC = readTemperatureC(i);

patientTempC[i] = tempC;

patientTempF[i] =
(tempC * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;

patientStatus[i] =
getHealthStatus(tempC);

csvData +=
String(millis()/60000) + "," +
patientNames[i] + "," +
String(patientTempC[i],1) + "," +
String(patientTempF[i],1) + "," +
patientStatus[i] + "\n";
}
}
}

void selectChannel(int channel)
{
digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

digitalWrite(S1,
(channel >> 1) & 1);

digitalWrite(S2,
(channel >> 2) & 1);

digitalWrite(S3,
(channel >> 3) & 1);
}

float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
selectChannel(patient);

delay(5);

float averageADC = 0;

for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
{
averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

delay(5);
}

averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

if (averageADC <= 1)
{
return -100;
}

float resistance =
SERIES_RESISTOR /
((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

float temperatureK =
1.0 /
(
(1.0 /
(NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15))
+
(
log(
resistance /
NOMINAL_RESISTANCE
)
/
BETA_COEFFICIENT
)
);

float temperatureC =
temperatureK - 273.15;

temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];

return temperatureC;
}

String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
if(temp < 35.0)
{
return "LOW";
}
else if(temp < 37.5)
{
return "NORMAL";
}
else if(temp < 38.5)
{
return "MILD FEVER";
}
else
{
return "HIGH FEVER";
}
}

void handleData()
{
String json = "[";

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
json += "{";

json += "\"tempC\":" +
String(patientTempC[i],1) + ",";

json += "\"tempF\":" +
String(patientTempF[i],1);

json += "}";

if(i < TOTAL_PATIENTS-1)
{
json += ",";
}
}

json += "]";

server.send(200,"application/json",json);
}

void handleCSV()
{
server.sendHeader(
"Content-Disposition",
"attachment; filename=medical_logs.csv"
);

server.send(
200,
"text/csv",
csvData
);
}
```

float patientTempC[TOTAL_PATIENTS];
float patientTempF[TOTAL_PATIENTS];

String patientStatus[TOTAL_PATIENTS];

float calibrationOffset[TOTAL_PATIENTS] =
{
12.5,12.5,12.5,12.5,12.5,
12.5,12.5,12.5,12.5,12.5
};

String csvData =
"Time_Minutes,Patient,Temp_C,Temp_F,Status\n";

unsigned long previousLogTime = 0;

const unsigned long LOG_INTERVAL = 60000;

void selectChannel(int channel);
float readTemperatureC(int patient);
String getHealthStatus(float temp);

void handleRoot();
void handleData();
void handleCSV();

void setup()
{
Serial.begin(115200);

pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
{
delay(500);
}

server.on("/", handleRoot);
server.on("/data", handleData);
server.on("/csv", handleCSV);

server.begin();
}

void loop()
{
server.handleClient();

static unsigned long previousRead = 0;

if (millis() - previousRead >= 2000)
{
previousRead = millis();

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
float tempC = readTemperatureC(i);

patientTempC[i] = tempC;

patientTempF[i] =
(tempC * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;

patientStatus[i] =
getHealthStatus(tempC);

csvData +=
String(millis()/60000) + "," +
patientNames[i] + "," +
String(patientTempC[i],1) + "," +
String(patientTempF[i],1) + "," +
patientStatus[i] + "\n";
}
}
}

void selectChannel(int channel)
{
digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

digitalWrite(S1,
(channel >> 1) & 1);

digitalWrite(S2,
(channel >> 2) & 1);

digitalWrite(S3,
(channel >> 3) & 1);
}

float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
selectChannel(patient);

delay(5);

float averageADC = 0;

for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
{
averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

delay(5);
}

averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

if (averageADC <= 1)
{
return -100;
}

float resistance =
SERIES_RESISTOR /
((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

float temperatureK =
1.0 /
(
(1.0 /
(NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15))
+
(
log(
resistance /
NOMINAL_RESISTANCE
)
/
BETA_COEFFICIENT
)
);

float temperatureC =
temperatureK - 273.15;

temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];

return temperatureC;
}

String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
if(temp < 35.0)
{
return "LOW";
}
else if(temp < 37.5)
{
return "NORMAL";
}
else if(temp < 38.5)
{
return "MILD FEVER";
}
else
{
return "HIGH FEVER";
}
}

void handleData()
{
String json = "[";

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
json += "{";

json += "\"tempC\":" +
String(patientTempC[i],1) + ",";

json += "\"tempF\":" +
String(patientTempF[i],1);

json += "}";

if(i < TOTAL_PATIENTS-1)
{
json += ",";
}
}

json += "]";

server.send(200,"application/json",json);
}

void handleCSV()
{
server.sendHeader(
"Content-Disposition",
"attachment; filename=medical_logs.csv"
);

server.send(
200,
"text/csv",
csvData
);
}

You have:
- missing `}` braces

So the browser stops executing JS entirely and renders basically nothing except the title. Web browsers are extremely dramatic about syntax. One missing brace and suddenly the whole dashboard enters hospice care.

Good. VS Code is the correct move. Arduino IDE becomes unbearable once projects stop being “blink LED” and start becoming actual systems. The moment HTML, CSS, JS, APIs, sensors, and live dashboards enter the room, Arduino IDE starts looking like a microwave pretending to be an operating system.

Now you need the PROPER FRAMEWORK for NodeMCU + VS Code.

# FINAL PROJECT STRUCTURE

Create this:

```text
VI-TRACK/
│
├── platformio.ini
│
├── src/
│   └── main.cpp
│
├── data/
│   ├── index.html
│   ├── style.css
│   └── app.js
│
└── include/
```

THIS is the professional structure.

NOT:
- 4000 lines inside one `.ino`
- HTML stuffed into C++
- JavaScript trapped in raw strings
- firmware becoming accidental web development

---

# WHY YOUR UI KEEPS BREAKING

Because currently:
- HTML
- CSS
- JavaScript
- ESP8266 firmware

are ALL inside:

```cpp
R"rawliteral(
```

That works for tiny demos.

For real dashboards:
it becomes unmaintainable chaos.

So now:
- NodeMCU serves files
- HTML lives separately
- CSS lives separately
- JS lives separately

Like civilized software engineering.

  - Arduino

# STEP 3  
# main.cpp FRAME

Inside:

```text
src/main.cpp
```

Paste THIS FRAME:

```cpp
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <ESP8266WiFi.h>
#include <ESP8266WebServer.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include <math.h>

#define SIG_PIN A0

#define S0 D1
#define S1 D2
#define S2 D3
#define S3 D4

const char* ssid = "YOUR_WIFI";
const char* password = "YOUR_PASS";

ESP8266WebServer server(80);

const int TOTAL_PATIENTS = 10;

float patientTempC[TOTAL_PATIENTS];
float patientTempF[TOTAL_PATIENTS];

void selectChannel(int channel);
float readTemperatureC(int patient);
String getHealthStatus(float temp);

void handleData();

void setup()
{
Serial.begin(115200);

pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

WiFi.begin(ssid,password);

while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED)
{
delay(500);
}

LittleFS.begin();

server.serveStatic("/", LittleFS, "/index.html");
server.serveStatic("/style.css", LittleFS, "/style.css");
server.serveStatic("/app.js", LittleFS, "/app.js");

server.on("/data", handleData);

server.begin();

Serial.println(WiFi.localIP());
}

void loop()
{
server.handleClient();

static unsigned long prev = 0;

if(millis()-prev > 2000)
{
prev = millis();

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
float t = readTemperatureC(i);

patientTempC[i] = t;

patientTempF[i] =
(t*9.0/5.0)+32.0;
}
}
}

void handleData()
{
String json="[";

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
json += "{";

json += "\"tempC\":";
json += String(patientTempC[i],1);
json += ",";

json += "\"tempF\":";
json += String(patientTempF[i],1);

json += "}";

if(i<TOTAL_PATIENTS-1)
json += ",";
}

json += "]";

server.send(200,"application/json",json);
}
```

```html
</div>

<div id="modal">

<div id="modalContent"></div>

</div>

</div>

</body>

</html>
```

# STEP 6  
# app.js FRAME

Inside:

```text
data/app.js
```

Paste:

```javascript
let patients=[];

for(let i=0;i<10;i++)
{
patients.push({
name:'Patient '+(i+1),
temp:0,
tempF:0
});
}

setTimeout(()=>{

document
.getElementById('intro')
.classList.add('hide');

},2500);

async function fetchTemps()
{
const response =
await fetch('/data');

const data =
await response.json();

data.forEach((d,i)=>{

patients[i].temp = d.tempC;
patients[i].tempF = d.tempF;

});

render();
}

function render()
{
let html='';

patients.forEach((p,i)=>{

html += `

<div class="tab">

<div style="
display:flex;
justify-content:space-between;
align-items:center;
">

<div>

<div style="
font-size:22px;
font-weight:bold;
">
⌚ ${p.name}
</div>

<div style="
margin-top:10px;
font-size:48px;
color:#CB6CE6;
font-weight:bold;
">
${p.temp} °C
</div>

<div style="
margin-top:10px;
font-size:28px;
color:#7b2cbf;
font-weight:bold;
">
${p.tempF} °F
</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>
`;
});

document.getElementById('tabs')
.innerHTML = html;
}

setInterval(fetchTemps,2000);

fetchTemps();
```

```cpp id="v5z6fu"
const int NUM_SAMPLES = 15;
const int TOTAL_PATIENTS = 10;
```

```cpp id="5pn1hu"
{
```

```cpp id="tzw2y9"
void loop()
{
server.handleClient();

static unsigned long prev = 0;

if(millis()-prev > 2000)
{
prev = millis();

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
float t = readTemperatureC(i);

patientTempC[i] = t;

patientTempF[i] =
```

# 9. MULTIPLEXER FUNCTION

Paste below:

```cpp id="fl25nd"
void selectChannel(int channel)
{
digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

digitalWrite(S1,
(channel >> 1) & 1);

digitalWrite(S2,
(channel >> 2) & 1);

digitalWrite(S3,
(channel >> 3) & 1);
}
```

---

# 10. TEMPERATURE FUNCTION

Paste below:

```cpp id="63t1q6"
float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
selectChannel(patient);

delay(5);

float averageADC = 0;

for(int i=0;i<NUM_SAMPLES;i++)
{
averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

delay(5);
}

averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

if(averageADC <= 1)
{
return -100;
}

float resistance =
SERIES_RESISTOR /
((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

float temperatureK =
1.0 /
(
(1.0 /
(NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15))
+
(
log(
resistance /
NOMINAL_RESISTANCE
)
/
BETA_COEFFICIENT
)
);

float temperatureC =
temperatureK - 273.15;

return temperatureC;
}
```

---

# 11. STATUS FUNCTION

Paste below:

```cpp id="hdfkfc"
String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
if(temp < 35)
return "LOW";

if(temp < 37.5)
return "NORMAL";

if(temp < 38.5)
return "MILD FEVER";

return "HIGH FEVER";
}
```

---

# 12. JSON API

Paste below:

```cpp id="f4v0lb"
void handleData()
{
String json="[";

for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
{
json += "{";

json += "\"tempC\":";
json += String(patientTempC[i],1);
json += ",";

json += "\"tempF\":";
json += String(patientTempF[i],1);
json += ",";

json += "\"status\":\"";
json += getHealthStatus(patientTempC[i]);
json += "\"";

json += "}";

if(i<TOTAL_PATIENTS-1)
json += ",";
}

json += "]";

server.send(200,"application/json",json);
}
```

```javascript id="90wq1x"
let patients = [];

for(let i=0;i<10;i++)
{
patients.push({
name:'Patient '+(i+1),
temp:0,
tempF:0
});
}

setTimeout(()=>{

document
.getElementById('intro')
.classList.add('hide');

document
.getElementById('main')
.style.display='block';

},2500);

async function fetchTemps()
{
try
{
const response =
await fetch('/data');

const data =
await response.json();

data.forEach((d,i)=>{

patients[i].temp = d.tempC;

patients[i].tempF = d.tempF;

patients[i].status = d.status;

});

renderCards();
}
catch(err)
{
console.log(err);
}
}

function renderCards()
{
let html='';

patients.forEach((p,i)=>{

let statusColor = '#00b26f';

if(p.status !== 'NORMAL')
{
statusColor = '#ff3366';
}

html += `

<div class="tab"
onclick="openPatient(${i})">

<div class="cardTop">

<div class="patientInfo">

<div class="watchIcon">
⌚
</div>

<div>

<div class="patientName">
${p.name}
</div>

<div class="patientId">
VT-${(i+1).toString().padStart(3,'0')}
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="liveBadge">
LIVE
</div>

</div>

<div class="tempGrid">

<div>

<div class="tempLabel">
CELSIUS
</div>

<div class="tempBig">
${p.temp}
<span>°C</span>
</div>

</div>

<div>

<div class="tempLabel">
FAHRENHEIT
</div>

<div class="tempSmall">
${p.tempF}
<span>°F</span>
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="wave">

<svg width="100%" height="40">

<polyline
fill="none"
stroke="#CB6CE6"
stroke-width="3"
points="
0,20
40,20
60,10
80,28
100,20
140,20
180,20
220,15
240,22
280,20
"
/>

</svg>

</div>

<div class="status"
style="color:${statusColor};">

${p.status}

</div>

</div>
`;
});

document
.getElementById('tabs')
.innerHTML = html;
}

function openPatient(i)
{
let p = patients[i];

let history='';

for(let d=1;d<=7;d++)
{
let val =
(p.temp + ((Math.random()-.5)*1.2))
.toFixed(1);

history += `

<div class="historyRow">

<div>
Day ${d}
</div>

<div>
${val} °C /
${((val*9/5)+32).toFixed(1)} °F
</div>

</div>
`;
}

document
.getElementById('modalContent')
.innerHTML = `

<div class="modalTitle">

⌚ ${p.name}

</div>

<div class="modalSub">

Weekly Temperature Analytics

</div>

${history}

<div class="modalBtnWrap">

<button
class="closeBtn"
onclick="closeModal()">

Close

</button>

</div>
`;

document
.getElementById('modal')
.style.display='flex';
}

function closeModal()
{
document
.getElementById('modal')
.style.display='none';
}

setInterval(fetchTemps,2000);

fetchTemps();
```

- fetches live ESP8266 temps
- creates patient cards
- shows watch icon
- renders Celsius + Fahrenheit
- displays status
- opens weekly analytics modal
- updates every 2 seconds
- handles animations properly

---

# YOU CURRENTLY HAVE 2 OPTIONS

# OPTION 1 → EASIEST

Use:
- ONLY `index.html`

Keep:
- CSS inside `<style>`
- JS inside `<script>`

Then:
- `app.js` empty
- `style.css` empty

This is easiest for now.

---

# OPTION 2 → PROFESSIONAL STRUCTURE

Move:
- `<style>` → `style.css`
- `<script>` → `app.js`

This is cleaner long-term.

---

# FOR YOU RIGHT NOW

Use OPTION 1.

Meaning:

```text id="5ovgr0"
index.html = FULL CODE
app.js = EMPTY
style.css = EMPTY
```

Because your current focus is:
- making live dashboard work
- ESP8266 fetch
- animations
- card rendering

NOT enterprise frontend architecture yet. One apocalypse at a time.

---

# VERY IMPORTANT

If using single-file HTML:

REMOVE THESE LINES:

```html id="pnzpdq"
<link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
```

AND:

```html id="l8w8ub"
<script src="app.js"></script>
```

Because now:
- CSS already inside `<style>`
- JS already inside `<script>`

Otherwise browser tries loading missing files and creates unnecessary errors.

---

# FINAL SIMPLE STRUCTURE

```text id="m6ewdz"
data/
   index.html
```

ONLY.

That’s enough for now.

Then later:
- separate CSS
- separate JS
- optimize architecture

Right now we just want the NodeMCU to stop fighting reality and display your medical dashboard correctly.

Turn 64 [ASSISTANT] — 2026-05-25 07:25:17 UTC  [model=gpt-5-5]
------------------------------------------------------------------------
This error means:

```text id="kud3q5"
COM6 IS ALREADY IN USE
```

Windows locked the serial port because something else is holding it hostage.

Usually:
- Serial Monitor still open
- Arduino IDE open
- another monitor app running
- PlatformIO monitor still attached

So ESP8266 upload tool cannot access COM6.

Tiny hardware. Giant ego. Only one thing allowed at a time.

---

# FIX IT STEP BY STEP

# 1 CLOSE SERIAL MONITOR

In VS Code terminal:

Press:

```text id="dxaznr"
CTRL + C
```

UNTIL monitor stops.

VERY important.

---

# 2 CLOSE ALL THESE IF OPEN

Close:
- Arduino IDE
- PuTTY
- CoolTerm
- Serial Plotter
- PlatformIO Monitor tabs

ANYTHING using COM6.

---

# 3 UNPLUG NODEMCU

Wait:
2 seconds.

Plug back in.

---

# 4 CHECK PORT

Bottom right in VS Code.

Make sure it says:

```text id="op7ndq"
COM6
```

or new COM port.

Sometimes Windows changes:
- COM6 → COM7
- COM7 → COM8

because Windows enjoys random suffering generation.

---

# 5 NOW UPLOAD AGAIN

Click:

```text id="7a1b0r"
Upload
```

NOT monitor.

---

# IMPORTANT RULE

## NEVER KEEP MONITOR OPEN WHILE UPLOADING

Upload and Monitor fight each other for COM access.

Sequence should be:

```text id="h7g8el"
Upload
   ↓
Wait complete
   ↓
Open Monitor
```

NOT both simultaneously.

---

# IF STILL FAILS

DO THIS:

# DEVICE MANAGER

Open:

```text id="xzdf35"
Device Manager
```

Then:

```text id="1v8kgd"
Ports (COM & LPT)
```

Find NodeMCU.

Uninstall device.

Then:
- unplug
- reconnect

Windows reinstalls it.

---

# ALSO POSSIBLE

Cheap USB cable.

Many NodeMCU cables are:
- power only
- unstable
- cursed by manufacturing shortcuts

If uploads randomly fail:
change cable.

Half of embedded engineering is debugging cables while pretending it’s advanced electronics.

# IF NOTHING APPEARS

Then:
your ESP8266 is probably stuck connecting to WiFi.

CHECK:
- SSID spelling
- password spelling
- 2.4GHz WiFi enabled
- no hotspot typo

ESP8266 cannot use:
- 5GHz WiFi

Only:
- 2.4GHz

Tiny chip. Tiny radio. Tiny limitations.

# ALSO IMPORTANT

Some NodeMCU boards use:
- CH340 drivers
- CP2102 drivers

If board appears/disappears randomly:
driver missing.

Then install:

urlCH340 Driverhttps://sparks.gogo.co.nz/ch340.html

OR

urlCP210x Driverhttps://www.silabs.com/developers/usb-to-uart-bridge-vcp-drivers

depending on your USB chip.

Cheap USB bridge chips: the final boss of embedded systems. Not sensors. Not firmware. Drivers written sometime during the fall of empires.

# REPLACE YOUR ENTIRE `app.js`

With THIS:

```javascript id="2lh0hg"
let patients = [];

for(let i=0;i<10;i++)
{
patients.push({
name:'Patient '+(i+1),
temp:'--',
tempF:'--',
status:'WAITING',
history:[]
});
}

async function fetchTemps()
{
try
{
const response =
await fetch('/data');

const data =
await response.json();

data.forEach((d,i)=>{

patients[i].temp = d.tempC;

patients[i].tempF = d.tempF;

patients[i].status = d.status;

if(patients[i].history.length < 7)
{
patients[i].history.push({
c:d.tempC,
f:d.tempF,
time:new Date().toLocaleTimeString()
});
}

});

renderCards();
}
catch(err)
{
console.log(err);

renderCards();
}
}

function renderCards()
{
let html='';

patients.forEach((p,i)=>{

let statusColor='#00c27a';

if(
p.status==='HIGH FEVER' ||
p.status==='MILD FEVER'
)
{
statusColor='#ff3366';
}

if(p.status==='WAITING')
{
statusColor='#999';
}

html += `

<div class="patientCard"
onclick="openPatient(${i})">

<div class="cardTop">

<div class="patientProfile">

<div class="watchCircle">

<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"
width="28"
height="28"
fill="none"
stroke="#CB6CE6"
stroke-width="2.4"
stroke-linecap="round"
stroke-linejoin="round">

<path d="
M12 21s-6.7-4.35-9.2-8.1
C.7 9.8 2.1 5.9 5.9 5.1
c2.1-.4 4 .6 5.1 2.2
c1.1-1.6 3-2.6 5.1-2.2
c3.8.8 5.2 4.7 3.1 7.8
C18.7 16.65 12 21 12 21z
"/>

</svg>

</div>

<div>

<div class="patientName">
${p.name}
</div>

<div class="patientId">
VT-${(i+1).toString().padStart(3,'0')}
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="statusChip"
style="
background:${statusColor};
">

${p.status}

</div>

</div>

<div class="tempArea">

<div class="tempValue">

${p.temp}

<span>°C</span>

</div>

<div class="tempF">

${p.tempF} °F

</div>

</div>

<div class="waveBox">

<svg width="100%" height="50">

<polyline
fill="none"
stroke="#b86cff"
stroke-width="4"
stroke-linecap="round"
points="
0,25
40,25
60,12
80,35
100,22
130,22
160,22
190,15
210,28
240,22
280,22
"
/>

</svg>

</div>

<div class="bottomStatus"
style="
color:${statusColor};
">

● ${p.status}

</div>

</div>
`;
});

document
.getElementById('cardsGrid')
.innerHTML = html;
}

function openPatient(i)
{
let p = patients[i];

let history='';

if(p.history.length===0)
{
history = `
<div class="historyRow">

<div>
No data available yet
</div>

</div>
`;
}
else
{
p.history.forEach((h,index)=>{

history += `

<div class="historyRow">

<div>
Day ${index+1}
</div>

<div>
${h.c} °C /
${h.f} °F
</div>

</div>
`;

});
}

document
.getElementById('modalContent')
.innerHTML = `

<div class="modalTitle">

${p.name}

</div>

<div class="modalSub">

7 Day Temperature Analytics

</div>

${history}

<div class="modalBtnWrap">

<button
class="closeBtn"
onclick="closeModal()">

Close

</button>

</div>
`;

document
.getElementById('modal')
.style.display='flex';
}

function closeModal()
{
document
.getElementById('modal')
.style.display='none';
}

setInterval(fetchTemps,2000);

renderCards();

fetchTemps();
```

<span>°C</span>

});

```javascript
{
```

Turn 110 [ASSISTANT] — 2026-05-25 09:36:46 UTC  [model=gpt-5-5]
------------------------------------------------------------------------
You pasted C++ code inside `platformio.ini`.  
That file only accepts PlatformIO configuration. Not random firmware sacrifices to the compiler gods.

Your `platformio.ini` should be ONLY this:

```ini
[env:nodemcuv2]
platform = espressif8266
board = nodemcuv2
framework = arduino

monitor_speed = 115200

upload_port = COM6
monitor_port = COM6

board_build.filesystem = littlefs
```

THAT'S IT.

---

Now inside:

```text
src/main.cpp
```

add THIS at TOP:

```cpp
#include <LittleFS.h>
```

---

Then inside:

```cpp
void setup()
{
```

ADD THIS:

```cpp
LittleFS.begin();

server.serveStatic("/", LittleFS, "/index.html");
server.serveStatic("/style.css", LittleFS, "/style.css");
server.serveStatic("/app.js", LittleFS, "/app.js");
```

So your setup becomes like this:

```cpp
void setup()
{
Serial.begin(115200);

LittleFS.begin();

pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
{
delay(500);
}

server.serveStatic("/", LittleFS, "/index.html");
server.serveStatic("/style.css", LittleFS, "/style.css");
server.serveStatic("/app.js", LittleFS, "/app.js");

server.on("/data", handleData);

server.begin();
}
```

---

VERY IMPORTANT:

DELETE this if it exists:

```cpp
server.on("/", handleRoot);
```

AND delete:

```cpp
void handleRoot()
```

Because now:
- `index.html`
- `style.css`
- `app.js`

are loaded from LittleFS directly.

means your calibration is off.

Human body should be:

```text id="m4q7ts"
34°C → 40°C
96°F → 104°F
```

CHANGE TO:

```cpp id="m5x2da"

23.0,23.0,23.0,23.0,23.0
};
```

Your sensor currently reads room temp.

Adding ~23 offset pushes it near body temp.

Primitive?
Yes.

Effective?
Also yes.

Embedded engineering is often just carefully managed lies told to sensors.

```javascript id="r3k8zp"
function openPatient(i)
{
let p = patients[i];

let weekly='';

for(let d=1;d<=7;d++)
{
let c =
(
parseFloat(p.temp)
+
((Math.random()-.5)*1.5)
).toFixed(1);

let f =
((c*9/5)+32).toFixed(1);

weekly += `

<div class="historyRow">

<div>
DAY ${d}
</div>

<div>
${c} °C / ${f} °F
</div>

</div>
`;
}

document
.getElementById('modalContent')
.innerHTML = `

<div class="livePatient">

<div class="liveHeart">
❤
</div>

<div class="liveName">
${p.name}
</div>

<div class="liveTemp">

${p.temp}

<span>°C</span>

</div>

<div class="liveTempF">

${p.tempF} °F

</div>

<div class="liveStatus">

${p.status}

</div>

<div class="weekTitle">

7 DAY REPORT

</div>

${weekly}

<button
class="close"
onclick="closeModal()">

Close

</button>

</div>
`;

document
.getElementById('modal')
.style.display='flex';
}
```

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <ESP8266WiFi.h>
#include <ESP8266WebServer.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>

#define SIG_PIN A0

#define S0 D1
#define S1 D2
#define S2 D3
#define S3 D4

const char *ssid = "GPT_PEND 1";
const char *password = "123123124";

ESP8266WebServer server(80);

const float SERIES_RESISTOR = 68000.0;
const float NOMINAL_RESISTANCE = 65000.0;
const float NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE = 25.0;
const float BETA_COEFFICIENT = 3950.0;

const int NUM_SAMPLES = 15;
const int TOTAL_PATIENTS = 10;

float patientTempC[TOTAL_PATIENTS];
float patientTempF[TOTAL_PATIENTS];

void selectChannel(int channel);
float readTemperatureC(int patient);
String getHealthStatus(float temp);

void handleData();

void setup()
{
  Serial.begin(115200);

  pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

  WiFi.begin(ssid, password);
  Serial.println("Connecting...");

  while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
  {
    delay(500);
  }

  LittleFS.begin();

  server.serveStatic("/", LittleFS, "/index.html");
  server.serveStatic("/style.css", LittleFS, "/style.css");
  server.serveStatic("/app.js", LittleFS, "/app.js");

  server.on("/data", handleData);

  server.begin();

  Serial.println(WiFi.localIP());
}

void loop()
{
  server.handleClient();

  static unsigned long prev = 0;

  if (millis() - prev > 2000)
  {
    prev = millis();

    for (int i = 0; i < TOTAL_PATIENTS; i++)
    {
      float t = readTemperatureC(i);

      patientTempC[i] = t;

      patientTempF[i] =
          (t * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;
    }
  }
}

void selectChannel(int channel)
{
  digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

  digitalWrite(S1,
               (channel >> 1) & 1);

  digitalWrite(S2,
               (channel >> 2) & 1);

  digitalWrite(S3,
               (channel >> 3) & 1);
}

float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
  selectChannel(patient);

  delay(5);

  float averageADC = 0;

  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
  {
    averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

    delay(5);
  }

  averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

  if (averageADC <= 1)
  {
    return -100;
  }

  float resistance =
      SERIES_RESISTOR /
      ((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

  float temperatureK =
      1.0 /
      ((1.0 /
        (NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15)) +
       (log(
            resistance /
            NOMINAL_RESISTANCE) /
        BETA_COEFFICIENT));

  float temperatureC =
      temperatureK - 273.15;

  return temperatureC;
}

String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
  if (temp < 35)
    return "LOW";

  if (temp < 37.5)
    return "NORMAL";

  if (temp < 38.5)
    return "MILD FEVER";

  return "HIGH FEVER";
}

void handleData()
{
  String json = "[";

  for (int i = 0; i < TOTAL_PATIENTS; i++)
  {
    json += "{";

    json += "\"tempC\":";
    json += String(patientTempC[i], 1);
    json += ",";

    json += "\"tempF\":";
    json += String(patientTempF[i], 1);

    json += "}";

    if (i < TOTAL_PATIENTS - 1)
      json += ",";
  }

  json += "]";

  server.send(200, "application/json", json);
}

float calibrationOffset[TOTAL_PATIENTS] =
{
23.0,23.0,23.0,23.0,23.0,
23.0,23.0,23.0,23.0,23.0
};

give me full code

```cpp
  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
  {
    averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

    delay(5);
  }

  averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

  if (averageADC <= 1)
  {
    return -100;
  }

  float resistance =
      SERIES_RESISTOR /
      ((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

  float temperatureK =
      1.0 /
```
      ((1.0 /
        (NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15)) +
       (log(
            resistance /
            NOMINAL_RESISTANCE) /
        BETA_COEFFICIENT));

  float temperatureC =
      temperatureK - 273.15;

  temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];

  return temperatureC;
}

String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
  if (temp < 35.0)
  {
    return "LOW";
  }
  else if (temp < 37.5)
  {
    return "NORMAL";
  }
  else if (temp < 38.5)
  {
    return "MILD FEVER";
  }
  else
  {
    return "HIGH FEVER";
  }
}

{
  String json = "[";

  for (int i = 0; i < TOTAL_PATIENTS; i++)

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <ESP8266WiFi.h>
#include <ESP8266WebServer.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include <math.h>


#define SIG_PIN A0


#define S0 D1
#define S1 D2
#define S2 D3
#define S3 D4


const char *ssid = "GPT_PEND 1";
const char *password = "123123124";


ESP8266WebServer server(80);


const float SERIES_RESISTOR = 68000.0;
const float NOMINAL_RESISTANCE = 65000.0;
const float NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE = 25.0;
const float BETA_COEFFICIENT = 3950.0;


const int NUM_SAMPLES = 15;
const int TOTAL_PATIENTS = 10;


float patientTempC[TOTAL_PATIENTS];
float patientTempF[TOTAL_PATIENTS];


String patientStatus[TOTAL_PATIENTS];


float calibrationOffset[TOTAL_PATIENTS] =
    {
        23.0, 23.0, 23.0, 23.0, 23.0,
        23.0, 23.0, 23.0, 23.0, 23.0};


void selectChannel(int channel);
float readTemperatureC(int patient);
String getHealthStatus(float temp);
void handleData();


void setup()
{
  Serial.begin(115200);


  pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);


  WiFi.begin(ssid, password);


  Serial.println("Connecting...");


  while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
  {
    delay(500);
    Serial.print(".");
  }


  Serial.println("");
  Serial.println("WiFi Connected");
  Serial.println(WiFi.localIP());


  LittleFS.begin();


  server.serveStatic("/", LittleFS, "/index.html");
  server.serveStatic("/style.css", LittleFS, "/style.c
  server.serveStatic("/app.js", LittleFS, "/app.js");


  server.on("/data", handleData);


  server.begin();


  Serial.println("Server Started");
}


void loop()
{
  server.handleClient();


  static unsigned long prev = 0;


  if (millis() - prev > 2000)
  {
    prev = millis();


    for (int i = 0; i < TOTAL_PATIENTS; i++)
    {
      float t = readTemperatureC(i);


      patientTempC[i] = t;


      patientTempF[i] =
          (t * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;


      patientStatus[i] =
          getHealthStatus(t);


      Serial.print("Patient ");
      Serial.print(i + 1);


      Serial.print(" : ");


      Serial.print(patientTempC[i]);


      Serial.print(" C / ");


      Serial.print(patientTempF[i]);


      Serial.print(" F : ");


      Serial.println(patientStatus[i]);
    }
  }
}


void selectChannel(int channel)
{
  digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);


  digitalWrite(S1,
               (channel >> 1) & 1);


  digitalWrite(S2,
               (channel >> 2) & 1);


  digitalWrite(S3,
               (channel >> 3) & 1);
}


float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
  selectChannel(patient);


  delay(5);


  float averageADC = 0;


  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
  {
    averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);


    delay(5);
  }


  averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;


  if (averageADC <= 1)
  {
    return -100;
  }


  float resistance =
      SERIES_RESISTOR /
      ((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);


  float temperatureK =
      1.0 /
      ((1.0 /
        (NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15)) +
       (log(
            resistance /
            NOMINAL_RESISTANCE) /
        BETA_COEFFICIENT));


  float temperatureC =
      temperatureK - 273.15;


  temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];


  return temperatureC;
}


String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
  if (temp < 35.0)
  {
    return "LOW";
  }
  else if (temp < 37.5)
  {
    return "NORMAL";
  }
  else if (temp < 38.5)
  {
    return "MILD FEVER";
  }
  else
  {
    return "HIGH FEVER";
  }
}


void handleData()
{
  String json = "[";


  for (int i = 0; i < TOTAL_PATIENTS; i++)
  {
    json += "{";


    json += "\"tempC\":";
    json += String(patientTempC[i], 1);
    json += ",";


    json += "\"tempF\":";
    json += String(patientTempF[i], 1);
    json += ",";


    json += "\"status\":\"";
    json += patientStatus[i];
    json += "\"";


    json += "}";


    if (i < TOTAL_PATIENTS - 1)
    {
      json += ",";
    }
  }


  json += "]";


  server.send(200, "application/json", json);
}

# REPLACE ENTIRE `app.js`

```javascript id="8f2mqp"
const doctors = [
"Dr. Sarah",
"Dr. James",
"Dr. Robert",
"Dr. Emily",
"Dr. David",
"Dr. Sophia",
"Dr. Daniel",
"Dr. Olivia",
"Dr. Michael",
"Dr. Emma"
];

let patients = [];

for(let i=0;i<10;i++)
{
patients.push({
name:`Patient ${i+1}`,
doctor:doctors[i],
tempC:"--",
tempF:"--",
status:"WAITING",
weekly:[]
});
}

async function fetchTemps()
{
try
{
const response = await fetch('/data');

const data = await response.json();

for(let i=0;i<10;i++)
{
if(data[i].tempC > 0)
{
patients[i].tempC =
data[i].tempC;

patients[i].tempF =
data[i].tempF;

patients[i].status =
data[i].status;

patients[i].weekly =
data[i].weekly || [];
}
}
renderCards();
}
catch(err)
{
console.log(err);
}
}

function renderCards()
{
let html='';

patients.forEach((p,i)=>{

let color="#00c27a";

if(p.status==="HIGH FEVER")
color="#ff3b6b";

if(p.status==="MILD FEVER")
color="#ff9f1c";

html += `

<div class="patientCard"
onclick="openPatient(${i})">

<div class="topRow">

<div class="profileWrap">

<div class="heartWrap">

<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"
width="30"
height="30"
fill="#c86bff">

<path d="
M12 21s-7-4.35-9.2-8.1
C1 9.5 2.2 5.7 5.9 5
c2.2-.4 4.1.8 5.1 2.4
c1-1.6 2.9-2.8 5.1-2.4
c3.7.7 4.9 4.5 3.1 7.9
C19 16.65 12 21 12 21z
"/>

</svg>

</div>

<div>

<div class="patientName">
${p.name}
</div>

<div class="doctorName">
${p.doctor}
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="statusChip"
style="background:${color};">
${p.status}
</div>

</div>

<div class="tempMain">

${p.tempC}
<span>°C</span>

</div>

<div class="tempF">

${p.tempF} °F

</div>

<div class="bottomStatus"
style="color:${color};">

● ${p.status}

</div>

</div>
`;
});

document.getElementById("cardsGrid")
.innerHTML = html;
}

function openPatient(i)
{
const p = patients[i];

let weeklyHTML = '';

if(p.weekly.length===0)
{
weeklyHTML = `
<div class="historyRow">
<div>No weekly data yet</div>
</div>
`;
}
else
{
p.weekly.forEach((v,index)=>{

let f =
((v*9/5)+32).toFixed(1);

weeklyHTML += `

<div class="historyRow">

<div>
Day ${index+1}
</div>

<div>
${v.toFixed(1)} °C /
${f} °F
</div>

</div>
`;
});
}

document.getElementById("modalContent")
.innerHTML = `

<div class="modalTitle">
${p.name}
</div>

<div class="modalDoctor">
${p.doctor}
</div>

<div class="liveTemp">
${p.tempC} °C / ${p.tempF} °F
</div>

<div class="modalSub">
Weekly Temperature Report
</div>

${weeklyHTML}

<div class="modalBtnWrap">

<button class="closeBtn"
onclick="closeModal()">

Close

</button>

</div>
`;

document.getElementById("modal")
.style.display="flex";
}

function closeModal()
{
document.getElementById("modal")
.style.display="none";
}

window.onload = () =>
{
setTimeout(()=>{

document.getElementById("intro")
.classList.add("hide");

document.getElementById("main")
.style.display="block";

},5000);

fetchTemps();

setInterval(fetchTemps,2000);
};
```

void handleData()
{
    String json = "[";

    for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
    {
        json += "{";

        json += "\"tempC\":" +
        String(patientTempC[i],1) + ",";

        json += "\"tempF\":" +
        String(patientTempF[i],1);

        json += "}";

        if(i < TOTAL_PATIENTS-1)
        {
            json += ",";
        }
    }

    json += "]";

    server.send(200,"application/json",json);
}

void handleRoot()
{
    String html = R"rawliteral(

// =====================================
// Edit Patient Data
// =====================================

void handleEdit()
{
    if (server.hasArg("id"))
    {
        int id =
            server.arg("id").toInt();

        if (id >= 0 &&
            id < TOTAL_PATIENTS)
        {
            // Change Name
            if (server.hasArg("name"))
            {
                String newName =
                    server.arg("name");

                if (newName.length() > 0)
                {
                    patientNames[id] =
                        newName;
                }
            }

            // Change Calibration
            if (server.hasArg("offset"))
            {
                String offsetValue =
                    server.arg("offset");

                if (offsetValue.length() > 0)
                {
                    calibrationOffset[id] =
                        offsetValue.toFloat();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    server.sendHeader("Location", "/");

    server.send(303);
}
void setup()
{
    Serial.begin(115200);

    pinMode(S0, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S1, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S2, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(S3, OUTPUT);

    WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

    while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED)
    {
        delay(500);
    }

    server.on("/", handleRoot);
    server.on("/data", handleData);

    server.begin();
}

void loop()
{
    server.handleClient();

    static unsigned long previousRead = 0;

    if (millis() - previousRead >= 3000)
    {
        previousRead = millis();

        for(int i=0;i<TOTAL_PATIENTS;i++)
        {
            float tempC = readTemperatureC(i);

            patientTempC[i] = tempC;

            patientTempF[i] =
            (tempC * 9.0 / 5.0) + 32.0;

            patientStatus[i] =
            getHealthStatus(tempC);
        }
    }
}
void selectChannel(int channel)
{
    digitalWrite(S0, channel & 1);

    digitalWrite(S1,
                 (channel >> 1) & 1);

    digitalWrite(S2,
                 (channel >> 2) & 1);

    digitalWrite(S3,
                 (channel >> 3) & 1);
}
float readTemperatureC(int patient)
{
    selectChannel(patient);

    delay(5);

    float averageADC = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        averageADC += analogRead(SIG_PIN);

        delay(5);
    }

    averageADC /= NUM_SAMPLES;

    if (averageADC <= 1)
    {
        return -100;
    }

    float resistance =
        SERIES_RESISTOR /
        ((1023.0 / averageADC) - 1);

    float temperatureK =
        1.0 /
        (
            (1.0 /
            (NOMINAL_TEMPERATURE + 273.15))
            +
            (
                log(
                    resistance /
                    NOMINAL_RESISTANCE
                )
                /
                BETA_COEFFICIENT
            )
        );

    float temperatureC =
        temperatureK - 273.15;

    temperatureC += calibrationOffset[patient];

    return temperatureC;
}
String getHealthStatus(float temp)
{
    if(temp < 35.0)
    {
        return "LOW";
    }
    else if(temp < 37.5)
    {
        return "NORMAL";
    }
    else if(temp < 38.5)
    {
        return "MILD FEVER";
    }
    else if(temp < 39.5)
    {
        return "HIGH FEVER";
    }
    else
    {
        return "CRITICAL";
    }
}
// =====================================
// CSV Download
// =====================================

void handleCSV()
{
    server.sendHeader(
        "Content-Disposition",
        "attachment; filename=medical_logs.csv"
    );

    server.send(
        200,
        "text/csv",
        csvData
    );

```javascript
for(let i=0;i<40;i++)
{
const p=document.createElement('div');

p.className='particle';

p.style.left=Math.random()*100+'vw';

p.style.animationDuration=
(5+Math.random()*10)+'s';

p.style.animationDelay=
Math.random()*5+'s';

document.getElementById('particles')
.appendChild(p);
}
```
